## 5. The queue, in order
1. ON EACH SEAT'S MESSAGE: verify its tip on origin before acting; rule what it asks; route what it names to the holder by message; when a seat hands back at its mark, seat a successor on the same lane (the generator `docs/chair/2026-11-14/probes/CHAIR/gen_briefs.py` holds every brief's source; a successor's brief is a new entry).
2. MERGE-15's READY TO LAND: read the classing; the word to land; then section 2's acts.
3. WHEN A LANE IS FINAL: a fresh reader reads it (small stages batched in one reader; the powers lane as a diff from 96a6ced46; the defence lane with the capacity commits as their own stage; the data lane diff-only; the corpus lane's new lines whole); round-three lanes are joined before they land; the merge seat lands each lane read CLEAR.
4. THE VEIL: when VEIL-10 names its final bytes, a FRESH SECURITY READER on the stronger tier (the plant library first, then the last review's section 8); ONLY ON ITS CLEAR tell the owner where the 212 pack is.
5. AFTER THE MERGES, one seat: the fourth handoff's section 5 item 7 and its section 4's FOR THE JOIN list.
6. THE CLOSING ACTS (the tuning; the site inventory ruled once; the carried reds); the measuring seat's command at zero on a fresh tree; THE FRESH ROUND OF THIRTY read thoroughly (THE BAR: no high and no medium false sentence); one whole check; CI; a draft pull request ONLY when the owner asks; the merge and the deploy are the owner's.

## 6. The chair's tools and this chair's lessons
- The recorder `docs/chair/2026-11-13/probes/CHAIR/rec.py` (run from a checkout of the chair's lane with `npm ci` done and the ledger ref fetched; give it your own trailer in CHAIR_TRAILER). `filereview.py` and `filebrief.py` name the earlier chair's paths and session: read before use.
- A HASH IS NEVER TYPED FROM MEMORY OR EXTENDED BY A GUESSED CHARACTER: twice on 2026-11-14 the chair wrote a wrong ninth character into a record (632dd125f for 632dd1259; a19a0ee1c for a19a0ee1b), each corrected in the next record. Hashes are read from git by the script that writes the text, and a record is checked before it is filed.
- A brief is a file on the chair's lane before its seat exists; the seat's first message only points at it and repeats the core.
